<file path="src/backtest/test_multi_portfolio.py">
"""Проверки портфеля с позицией на каждом рынке (plan.md, 5.5).

Запуск: uv run pytest src/backtest/test_multi_portfolio.py
"""

from datetime import timedelta

import polars as pl
import pytest

from backtest.portfolio import (
    BUSY,
    NO_CAPITAL,
    PORTFOLIO_MARGIN,
    TAKEN,
    ZERO_SIZE,
    MultiRules,
    run_multi_portfolio,
    run_portfolio,
)
from backtest.report import build_report, market_timeline
from backtest.test_portfolio import (
    CAPITAL,
    FREE,
    START,
    bar,
    flat_bars,
    make_bars,
    make_signals,
    market,
    signal,
    status_of,
    trend_bars,
)


def rules(**fractions: float) -> MultiRules:
    return MultiRules(risk_fractions=fractions)


def test_positions_on_different_markets_overlap():
    # Оба рынка доходят до цели 110 на свече 45. Риск a — 1%, b — 0.5%:
    # стоп в 5 пунктах → 2 000 и 1 000 контрактов.
    markets = {"a": market(FREE, trend_bars()), "b": market(FREE, trend_bars())}
    signals = make_signals([signal("a", 0, 95.0, 110.0), signal("b", 0, 95.0, 110.0)])
    result = run_multi_portfolio(signals, markets, CAPITAL, rules(a=0.01, b=0.005))

    assert status_of(result, "a", 0)["status"] == TAKEN
    assert status_of(result, "b", 0)["status"] == TAKEN
    a, b = result.trades
    assert (a.size.contracts, b.size.contracts) == (2_000, 1_000)
    assert a.trade.net_pnl == pytest.approx(20_000.0)
    assert b.trade.net_pnl == pytest.approx(10_000.0)
    assert result.final_capital == pytest.approx(1_030_000.0)

    report = build_report(result, market_timeline([trend_bars()]))
    curve = report.curve
    # На свече 30 обе сделки открыты: закрытие 102, вход 100.
    row = curve.filter(pl.col("timestamp") == START + timedelta(minutes=30)).row(
        0, named=True
    )
    assert row["positions"] == 2
    assert row["equity"] == pytest.approx(1_000_000.0 + 2_000 * 2 + 1_000 * 2)
    assert curve["positions"].max() == 2
    assert curve["equity"][-1] == pytest.approx(1_030_000.0)


def test_same_market_busy_until_its_exit_bar():
    signals = make_signals(
        [
            signal("a", 0, 95.0, 110.0),
            signal("a", 15, 95.0, 110.0),
            signal("a", 30, 95.0, 110.0),
            signal("a", 45, 105.0, 120.0),
        ]
    )
    result = run_multi_portfolio(
        signals, {"a": market(FREE, trend_bars())}, CAPITAL, rules(a=0.01)
    )
    for minute in (15, 30):
        row = status_of(result, "a", minute)
        assert row["status"] == BUSY
        assert row["trade_id"] == 0
    # Сделка закрылась на свече 45: сигнал по её закрытию исполняется.
    assert status_of(result, "a", 45)["status"] == TAKEN


def test_one_market_matches_single_position_portfolio():
    # С одним рынком оба портфеля обязаны дать одно и то же.
    signals = make_signals(
        [
            signal("a", 0, 95.0, 110.0),
            signal("a", 15, 95.0, 110.0),
            signal("a", 45, 105.0, 120.0),
        ]
    )
    markets = {"a": market(FREE, trend_bars())}
    single = run_portfolio(signals, markets, CAPITAL)
    multi = run_multi_portfolio(signals, markets, CAPITAL, rules(a=0.01))
    assert multi.trade_log().equals(single.trade_log())
    assert multi.signals["status"].to_list() == single.signals["status"].to_list()
    assert multi.final_capital == pytest.approx(single.final_capital)


def test_size_uses_only_trades_closed_by_the_signal():
    # a закрывается по цели на свече 45 (+20 000 ₽). Сигнал b на 15 — от
    # стартового капитала: a ещё открыта. Сигнал c на 45 — уже от 1 020 000.
    markets = {
        "a": market(FREE, trend_bars()),
        "b": market(FREE, flat_bars()),
        "c": market(FREE, flat_bars()),
    }
    signals = make_signals(
        [
            signal("a", 0, 95.0, 110.0),
            signal("b", 15, 45.0, 60.0),
            signal("c", 45, 45.0, 60.0),
        ]
    )
    result = run_multi_portfolio(
        signals, markets, CAPITAL, rules(a=0.01, b=0.01, c=0.01)
    )
    _, b, c = result.trades
    assert b.capital_before == pytest.approx(CAPITAL)
    assert b.size.contracts == 2_000
    assert c.capital_before == pytest.approx(1_020_000.0)
    assert c.size.contracts == 2_040


def test_new_position_shrinks_to_free_margin():
    # Цена 50, ГО 10% → 5 ₽ на контракт, стоп в 5 пунктах. a: риск 30% →
    # 60 000 контрактов, ГО 300 000 ₽ (30%). b хочет столько же, свободно 20%:
    # 200 000 ÷ 5 = 40 000 контрактов.
    markets = {"a": market(FREE, flat_bars()), "b": market(FREE, flat_bars())}
    signals = make_signals([signal("a", 0, 45.0, 60.0), signal("b", 15, 45.0, 60.0)])
    result = run_multi_portfolio(signals, markets, CAPITAL, rules(a=0.3, b=0.3))

    a, b = result.trades
    assert a.size.contracts == 60_000
    assert b.size.contracts == 40_000
    assert b.size.limit == PORTFOLIO_MARGIN
    assert status_of(result, "b", 15)["limit"] == PORTFOLIO_MARGIN
    assert a.size.margin + b.size.margin == pytest.approx(0.5 * CAPITAL)
    # 1R — риск того, что открыто: 40 000 × 5 ₽.
    assert b.size.risk == pytest.approx(200_000.0)


def test_simultaneous_signals_shrink_in_the_same_share():
    # Обе хотят по 300 000 ₽ ГО, свободно 500 000: каждая — 5/6 размера.
    markets = {"a": market(FREE, flat_bars()), "b": market(FREE, flat_bars())}
    signals = make_signals([signal("a", 0, 45.0, 60.0), signal("b", 0, 45.0, 60.0)])
    result = run_multi_portfolio(signals, markets, CAPITAL, rules(a=0.3, b=0.3))

    assert [item.size.contracts for item in result.trades] == [50_000, 50_000]
    assert {item.size.limit for item in result.trades} == {PORTFOLIO_MARGIN}


def test_no_free_margin_skips_signal():
    # a занимает все 50% ГО: у b 0 контрактов.
    markets = {"a": market(FREE, flat_bars()), "b": market(FREE, flat_bars())}
    signals = make_signals([signal("a", 0, 45.0, 60.0), signal("b", 15, 45.0, 60.0)])
    result = run_multi_portfolio(signals, markets, CAPITAL, rules(a=0.5, b=0.3))

    row = status_of(result, "b", 15)
    assert row["status"] == ZERO_SIZE
    assert row["limit"] == PORTFOLIO_MARGIN
    assert row["contracts"] == 0
    assert len(result.trades) == 1


def test_trading_stops_when_realized_capital_is_gone():
    # Гэп со 100 до 70 мимо стопа 99.9: 50 000 контрактов теряют 1.5 млн ₽.
    gap = make_bars(
        [
            bar(0, 100.0, 100.5, 99.5, 100.0),
            bar(15, 100.0, 100.5, 99.95, 100.0),
            *[bar(m, 70.0, 71.0, 69.0, 70.0) for m in range(30, 90, 15)],
        ]
    )
    markets = {"a": market(FREE, gap), "b": market(FREE, flat_bars())}
    signals = make_signals([signal("a", 0, 99.9, 110.0), signal("b", 45, 45.0, 60.0)])
    result = run_multi_portfolio(signals, markets, CAPITAL, rules(a=0.5, b=0.01))
    assert result.final_capital == pytest.approx(-500_000.0)
    assert status_of(result, "b", 45)["status"] == NO_CAPITAL


def test_risk_scale_multiplies_market_risk():
    # Доля a — 1%, стоп в 5 пунктах: 2 000 контрактов; с risk_scale 1.5 — 3 000,
    # с 0.5 — 1 000. Без столбца — множитель 1.
    for scale, contracts in ((1.5, 3_000), (0.5, 1_000)):
        signals = make_signals([signal("a", 0, 95.0, 110.0)]).with_columns(
            pl.lit(scale).alias("risk_scale")
        )
        result = run_multi_portfolio(
            signals, {"a": market(FREE, trend_bars())}, CAPITAL, rules(a=0.01)
        )
        assert result.trades[0].size.contracts == contracts


@pytest.mark.parametrize("scale", [0.0, -1.0, None])
def test_bad_risk_scale_rejected(scale):
    signals = make_signals([signal("a", 0, 95.0, 110.0)]).with_columns(
        pl.lit(scale, dtype=pl.Float64).alias("risk_scale")
    )
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="risk_scale"):
        run_multi_portfolio(
            signals, {"a": market(FREE, trend_bars())}, CAPITAL, rules(a=0.01)
        )


def test_every_market_needs_its_risk():
    signals = make_signals([signal("a", 0, 95.0, 110.0)])
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="доля риска"):
        run_multi_portfolio(
            signals, {"a": market(FREE, trend_bars())}, CAPITAL, rules(b=0.01)
        )

    "kwargs",
    [
        {"risk_fractions": {"a": 0.0}},
        {"risk_fractions": {"a": 1.0}},
        {"risk_fractions": {"a": 0.01}, "max_margin_fraction": 0.0},
    ],
)
def test_bad_rules_rejected(kwargs):
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        MultiRules(**kwargs)

<file path="src/backtest/test_portfolio.py">
"""Проверки портфеля с одной позицией. Запуск: uv run pytest src/backtest/test_portfolio.py"""

# бары в тестах — naive timestamp MSK, как в data/continuous_15m.
# ruff: noqa: DTZ001

from datetime import datetime, timedelta

import polars as pl
import pytest

from backtest.minutes import RawMinutes
from backtest.portfolio import (
    BUSY,
    LOST_TIE,
    NO_CAPITAL,
    NO_NEXT_BAR,
    TAKEN,
    ZERO_SIZE,
    MarketData,
    run_portfolio,
)
from backtest.position import LONG
from backtest.specs import ContractSpec

START = datetime(2026, 9, 10, 10)
CAPITAL = 1_000_000.0

# Без издержек: риск контракта = расстояние до стопа × рубли за пункт.
FREE = ContractSpec(
    asset="free", tick_size=0.1, exchange_fee_rate=0.0, slippage_ticks=0.0
)
# Проскальзывание 1 пункт на сторону: издержки контракта 2 ₽.
COSTLY = ContractSpec(asset="costly", tick_size=1.0, exchange_fee_rate=0.0)


def bar(
    minute: int,
    open_: float,
    high: float,
    low: float,
    close: float,
    point_value: float = 1.0,
    usual_volume: float = 1_000_000.0,
) -> dict:
    """Свеча; обычный объём по умолчанию огромный — лимит ликвидности не мешает."""
    return {
        "timestamp": START + timedelta(minutes=minute),
        "contract": "TSZ6",
        "open": open_,
        "high": high,
        "low": low,
        "close": close,
        "price_adjustment": 0.0,
        "is_first_bar_after_roll": False,
        "point_value": point_value,
        "point_value_known": point_value,
        "margin_rate": 0.1,
        "volume": 4000,
        "usual_volume": usual_volume,
    }


def make_bars(rows: list[dict]) -> pl.DataFrame:
    return pl.DataFrame(rows, infer_schema_length=None)


def trend_bars() -> pl.DataFrame:
    """Сигнал на 100, вход по 100 на свече 15, цель 110 задета на свече 45."""
    return make_bars(
        [
            bar(0, 100.0, 101.0, 99.0, 100.0),
            bar(15, 100.0, 102.0, 99.0, 101.0),
            bar(30, 101.0, 103.0, 100.0, 102.0),
            bar(45, 102.0, 111.0, 101.0, 110.0),
            bar(60, 110.0, 111.0, 109.0, 110.0),
            bar(75, 110.0, 111.0, 109.0, 110.0),
        ]
    )


def flat_bars(point_value: float = 1.0) -> pl.DataFrame:
    """Цена стоит на 50: лонг со стопом 45 и целью 60 доживает до конца ряда."""
    return make_bars(
        [bar(m, 50.0, 51.0, 49.0, 50.0, point_value) for m in range(0, 90, 15)]
    )


def market(spec: ContractSpec, bars: pl.DataFrame, minutes=None) -> MarketData:
    return MarketData(spec=spec, bars=bars, minutes=minutes)


def signal(
    name: str, minute: int, stop: float, target: float, max_bars: int | None = None
) -> dict:
    return {
        "market": name,
        "timestamp": START + timedelta(minutes=minute),
        "side": LONG,
        "stop": stop,
        "target": target,
        "max_bars": max_bars,
    }


def make_signals(rows: list[dict]) -> pl.DataFrame:
    return pl.DataFrame(rows, infer_schema_length=None)


def status_of(result, name: str, minute: int) -> dict:
    rows = result.signals.filter(
        (pl.col("market") == name)
        & (pl.col("timestamp") == START + timedelta(minutes=minute))
    )
    assert rows.height == 1
    return rows.row(0, named=True)


def two_markets() -> dict[str, MarketData]:
    return {"a": market(FREE, trend_bars()), "b": market(FREE, flat_bars())}


def test_busy_until_exit_bar_and_capital_carries_over():
    signals = make_signals(
        [
            signal("a", 0, 95.0, 110.0),
            signal("b", 15, 45.0, 60.0),
            signal("b", 30, 45.0, 60.0),
            signal("b", 45, 45.0, 60.0),
        ]
    )
    result = run_portfolio(signals, two_markets(), CAPITAL)

    assert status_of(result, "a", 0)["status"] == TAKEN
    # Сигналы во время сделки a — «занято», с номером мешавшей сделки.
    for minute in (15, 30):
        row = status_of(result, "b", minute)
        assert row["status"] == BUSY
        assert row["trade_id"] == 0
        assert row["contracts"] is None
    # Сделка a закрылась на свече 45: сигнал по её закрытию уже исполняется.
    assert status_of(result, "b", 45)["status"] == TAKEN
    assert status_of(result, "b", 45)["trade_id"] == 1

    first, second = result.trades
    # Риск 1% = 10 000 ₽, стоп в 5 пунктах по 1 ₽ → 2 000 контрактов.
    assert first.size.contracts == 2_000
    assert first.trade.exit_reason == "target"
    assert first.trade.net_pnl == pytest.approx(20_000.0)
    assert first.r_multiple == pytest.approx(2.0)
    # Вторая сделка — от капитала после первой: 10 200 ₽ ÷ 5 ₽ = 2 040.
    assert second.capital_before == pytest.approx(1_020_000.0)
    assert second.size.contracts == 2_040
    assert second.trade.entry_time == START + timedelta(minutes=60)
    assert result.final_capital == pytest.approx(1_020_000.0)


def test_cheaper_signal_wins_on_same_bar():
    markets = {"a": market(COSTLY, trend_bars()), "b": market(FREE, flat_bars())}
    signals = make_signals([signal("a", 0, 95.0, 110.0), signal("b", 0, 45.0, 60.0)])
    result = run_portfolio(signals, markets, CAPITAL)

    loser = status_of(result, "a", 0)
    assert loser["status"] == LOST_TIE
    assert loser["trade_id"] == 0
    # Издержки 2 ₽ при риске контракта 5 + 2 = 7 ₽.
    assert loser["cost_share"] == pytest.approx(2 / 7)
    winner = status_of(result, "b", 0)
    assert winner["status"] == TAKEN
    assert winner["cost_share"] == 0.0
    assert len(result.trades) == 1
    assert result.trades[0].trade.asset == "free"


@pytest.mark.parametrize(("order", "winner"), [("ab", "a"), ("ba", "b")])
def test_equal_costs_resolved_by_market_order(order, winner):
    data = two_markets()
    markets = {name: data[name] for name in order}
    signals = make_signals([signal("a", 0, 95.0, 110.0), signal("b", 0, 45.0, 60.0)])
    result = run_portfolio(signals, markets, CAPITAL)
    loser = "b" if winner == "a" else "a"
    assert status_of(result, winner, 0)["status"] == TAKEN
    assert status_of(result, loser, 0)["status"] == LOST_TIE


def test_zero_size_passes_turn_to_next_candidate():
    # b дешевле (без издержек), но стоп в 49 пунктах по 1 000 ₽ — 49 000 ₽
    # на контракт при бюджете 10 000 ₽: 0 контрактов, берётся a.
    markets = {
        "a": market(COSTLY, trend_bars()),
        "b": market(FREE, flat_bars(point_value=1_000.0)),
    }
    signals = make_signals([signal("a", 0, 95.0, 110.0), signal("b", 0, 1.0, 60.0)])
    result = run_portfolio(signals, markets, CAPITAL)

    skipped = status_of(result, "b", 0)
    assert skipped["status"] == ZERO_SIZE
    assert skipped["limit"] == "risk"
    assert skipped["contracts"] == 0
    assert skipped["trade_id"] is None
    assert status_of(result, "a", 0)["status"] == TAKEN
    assert result.trades[0].trade.asset == "costly"


def test_thin_entry_bar_passes_turn_to_next_candidate():
    # b дешевле, но свеча входа у b обычно почти пустая: 10% от 5 контрактов — 0.
    thin = make_bars(
        [
            bar(m, 50.0, 51.0, 49.0, 50.0, usual_volume=5.0 if m == 15 else 1e6)
            for m in range(0, 90, 15)
        ]
    )
    markets = {"a": market(COSTLY, trend_bars()), "b": market(FREE, thin)}
    signals = make_signals([signal("a", 0, 95.0, 110.0), signal("b", 0, 45.0, 60.0)])
    result = run_portfolio(signals, markets, CAPITAL)

    skipped = status_of(result, "b", 0)
    assert skipped["status"] == ZERO_SIZE
    assert skipped["limit"] == "volume"
    assert status_of(result, "a", 0)["status"] == TAKEN


def test_signal_on_last_bar_has_nothing_to_enter():
    signals = make_signals([signal("a", 75, 105.0, 120.0)])
    result = run_portfolio(signals, two_markets(), CAPITAL)
    assert status_of(result, "a", 75)["status"] == NO_NEXT_BAR
    assert result.trades == []
    assert result.final_capital == CAPITAL
    assert result.trade_log().is_empty()


def test_trading_stops_when_capital_is_gone():
    # Стоп в 0.1 пункта: по риску 100 000 контрактов, ГО ограничивает 50 000.
    # Гэп со 100 до 70 мимо стопа: убыток 1.5 млн ₽ при капитале 1 млн ₽.
    gap = make_bars(
        [
            bar(0, 100.0, 100.5, 99.5, 100.0),
            bar(15, 100.0, 100.5, 99.95, 100.0),
            *[bar(m, 70.0, 71.0, 69.0, 70.0) for m in range(30, 90, 15)],
        ]
    )
    markets = {"a": market(FREE, gap), "b": market(FREE, flat_bars())}
    signals = make_signals([signal("a", 0, 99.9, 110.0), signal("b", 45, 45.0, 60.0)])
    result = run_portfolio(signals, markets, CAPITAL)

    trade = result.trades[0]
    assert trade.size.limit == "margin"
    assert trade.size.contracts == 50_000
    assert trade.trade.exit_price == pytest.approx(70.0)
    assert result.final_capital == pytest.approx(-500_000.0)
    assert status_of(result, "b", 45)["status"] == NO_CAPITAL


def test_minutes_reach_the_simulator(tmp_path):
    # Свеча 45 задела и стоп 95, и цель 110; минутки: цель была раньше.
    bars = make_bars(
        [
            *trend_bars().head(3).iter_rows(named=True),
            bar(45, 102.0, 111.0, 94.0, 105.0),
            bar(60, 105.0, 106.0, 104.0, 105.0),
        ]
    )
    start = START + timedelta(minutes=45)
    pl.DataFrame(
        [
            {
                "timestamp": start,
                "open": 102.0,
                "high": 111.0,
                "low": 101.0,
                "close": 110.0,
            },
            {
                "timestamp": start + timedelta(minutes=1),
                "open": 110.0,
                "high": 110.0,
                "low": 94.0,
                "close": 105.0,
            },
        ]
    ).write_parquet(tmp_path / "TSZ6.parquet")
    signals = make_signals([signal("a", 0, 95.0, 110.0)])

    with_minutes = run_portfolio(
        signals, {"a": market(FREE, bars, RawMinutes(tmp_path))}, CAPITAL
    )
    without = run_portfolio(signals, {"a": market(FREE, bars)}, CAPITAL)
    assert with_minutes.trades[0].trade.exit_reason == "target"
    assert with_minutes.trades[0].trade.exit_resolution == "minutes"
    assert without.trades[0].trade.exit_reason == "stop"
    assert without.trades[0].trade.exit_resolution == "no_minutes"


def test_max_bars_reaches_the_simulator():
    signals = make_signals([signal("a", 0, 95.0, 110.0, max_bars=2)])
    result = run_portfolio(signals, two_markets(), CAPITAL)
    trade = result.trades[0].trade
    assert trade.exit_reason == "time"
    assert trade.exit_time == START + timedelta(minutes=30)


def test_max_bars_column_is_optional():
    signals = make_signals([signal("a", 0, 95.0, 110.0)]).drop("max_bars")
    result = run_portfolio(signals, two_markets(), CAPITAL)
    assert result.trades[0].trade.exit_reason == "target"


def test_hold_reaches_the_simulator():
    bars = (
        trend_bars()
        .with_row_index("_i")
        .with_columns(
            (pl.col("_i") == 2).alias("is_day_end"),
            pl.lit(False).alias("is_before_break"),
        )
    )
    signals = make_signals([signal("a", 0, 95.0, 110.0)]).with_columns(
        pl.lit("day").alias("hold")
    )
    result = run_portfolio(signals, {"a": market(FREE, bars.drop("_i"))}, CAPITAL)
    trade = result.trades[0].trade
    assert trade.exit_reason == "day_end"
    assert trade.exit_time == START + timedelta(minutes=30)
    # Следующий сигнал принимается с бара выхода, как после любой сделки.
    assert result.signals["hold"].to_list() == ["day"]


def test_hold_column_is_optional_and_empty_means_any():
    signals = make_signals([signal("a", 0, 95.0, 110.0)]).with_columns(
        pl.lit(None, dtype=pl.String).alias("hold")
    )
    result = run_portfolio(signals, two_markets(), CAPITAL)
    assert result.trades[0].trade.exit_reason == "target"


def test_moving_stop_and_empty_target_reach_the_simulator():
    signals = make_signals([signal("a", 0, 95.0, 110.0)]).with_columns(
        pl.lit(None, dtype=pl.Float64).alias("target"),
        pl.lit(1.0).alias("trail"),
    )
    result = run_portfolio(signals, two_markets(), CAPITAL)
    trade = result.trades[0].trade
    # Закрытие 101 на свече 15 → стоп 100, свеча 30 его задевает.
    assert trade.exit_reason == "trail"
    assert trade.exit_time == START + timedelta(minutes=30)
    assert trade.exit_price == pytest.approx(100.0)


def test_breakeven_column_reaches_the_simulator():
    signals = make_signals([signal("a", 0, 95.0, 110.0)]).with_columns(
        pl.lit(0.2).alias("breakeven_r")
    )
    result = run_portfolio(signals, two_markets(), CAPITAL)
    # Свеча 15 дошла до 102 (> 100 + 0.2 × 5): со свечи 30 стоп на входе 100,
    # её минимум 100 его задевает.
    trade = result.trades[0].trade
    assert trade.exit_reason == "breakeven"
    assert trade.exit_price == pytest.approx(100.0)


def test_signal_order_in_input_does_not_matter():
    rows = [
        signal("a", 0, 95.0, 110.0),
        signal("b", 15, 45.0, 60.0),
        signal("b", 45, 45.0, 60.0),
    ]
    forward = run_portfolio(make_signals(rows), two_markets(), CAPITAL)
    backward = run_portfolio(make_signals(rows[::-1]), two_markets(), CAPITAL)
    assert forward.signals.equals(backward.signals)
    assert forward.trade_log().equals(backward.trade_log())


def test_trade_log_and_volume_share():
    signals = make_signals([signal("a", 0, 95.0, 110.0)])
    result = run_portfolio(signals, two_markets(), CAPITAL)
    log = result.trade_log()
    assert log.height == 1
    row = log.row(0, named=True)
    assert row["market"] == "free"
    assert row["contracts"] == 2_000
    assert row["r_multiple"] == pytest.approx(2.0)
    # 2 000 контрактов при объёме свечи входа 4 000 — половина рынка.
    assert row["volume_share"] == pytest.approx(0.5)
    assert row["capital_after"] == pytest.approx(1_020_000.0)
    # Без издержек: low 99 бара входа — −1 пункт × 2 000 = −2 000 ₽ = −0.2R;
    # цель 110 — +10 × 2 000 = 20 000 ₽ = 2R.
    assert row["mae_r"] == pytest.approx(-0.2)
    assert row["mfe_r"] == pytest.approx(2.0)
    assert row["costs"] == 0.0
    assert row["pnl_before_costs"] == pytest.approx(row["net_pnl"])


def test_volume_share_without_volume_column():
    markets = {"a": market(FREE, trend_bars().drop("volume"))}
    result = run_portfolio(make_signals([signal("a", 0, 95.0, 110.0)]), markets)
    assert result.trades[0].entry_volume is None
    assert result.trades[0].volume_share is None


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("rows", "message"),
    [
        ([signal("x", 0, 95.0, 110.0)], "Нет данных рынков"),
        ([signal("a", 5, 95.0, 110.0)], "нет свечи сигнала"),
        ([signal("a", 0, 95.0, 110.0)] * 2, "Два сигнала"),
    ],
)
def test_bad_signals_rejected(rows, message):
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match=message):
        run_portfolio(make_signals(rows), two_markets(), CAPITAL)

    signals = make_signals([signal("a", 0, 95.0, 110.0)]).drop("stop")
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="нет столбцов"):

def test_unsorted_bars_rejected():

        run_portfolio(make_signals([signal("a", 0, 95.0, 110.0)]), markets)

    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="капитал"):

<file path="src/backtest/test_position.py">
"""Проверки симулятора одной позиции. Запуск: uv run pytest src/backtest/test_position.py"""

# бары в тестах — naive timestamp MSK, как в data/continuous_15m.
# ruff: noqa: DTZ001

import random
from dataclasses import replace
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from pathlib import Path

import polars as pl
import pytest

from backtest.markets import MARKETS, add_point_value
from backtest.minutes import RawMinutes
from backtest.position import LONG, SHORT, Signal, simulate_position
from backtest.specs import ContractSpec

# Рубли за пункт приходят из баров; в большинстве тестов 1 ₽ за пункт,
# поэтому результат в рублях равен результату в пунктах.
SPEC = ContractSpec(asset="TEST", tick_size=0.5, exchange_fee_rate=0.0001)
START = datetime(2026, 9, 10, 10)


def make_bars(rows: list[dict]) -> pl.DataFrame:
    return pl.DataFrame(rows, infer_schema_length=None)


def bar(
    minute: int,
    open_: float,
    high: float,
    low: float,
    close: float,
    adjustment: float = 0.0,
    roll: bool = False,
    contract: str = "TSZ6",
    point_value: float | None = 1.0,
) -> dict:
    return {
        "timestamp": START + timedelta(minutes=minute),
        "contract": contract,
        "open": open_,
        "high": high,
        "low": low,
        "close": close,
        "price_adjustment": adjustment,
        "is_first_bar_after_roll": roll,
        "point_value": point_value,
    }


def long_signal(**kwargs) -> Signal:
    params = {"stop": 95.0, "target": 110.0, **kwargs}
    return Signal(side=LONG, **params)


def test_long_target_fills_by_next_open_with_costs():
    bars = make_bars(
        [
            bar(0, 99.0, 101.0, 98.0, 100.0),
            bar(15, 101.0, 103.0, 100.0, 102.0),
            bar(30, 102.0, 106.0, 101.0, 105.0),
            bar(45, 108.0, 111.0, 107.0, 110.0),
        ]
    )
    trade = simulate_position(SPEC, bars, 0, long_signal())
    assert trade.entry_price == pytest.approx(101.5)
    assert trade.exit_price == pytest.approx(109.5)
    assert trade.exit_reason == "target"
    assert trade.exit_resolution == "bar"
    assert trade.bars_held == 3
    assert trade.gross_pnl == pytest.approx(8.0)
    assert trade.commissions == pytest.approx(0.0211)
    assert trade.roll_costs == 0.0
    assert trade.net_pnl == pytest.approx(8.0 - 0.0211)


def test_stop_has_priority_in_bar_with_both_levels_without_minutes():
    bars = make_bars(
        [
            bar(0, 99.0, 101.0, 98.0, 100.0),
            bar(15, 101.0, 103.0, 100.0, 102.0),
            bar(30, 102.0, 106.0, 101.0, 105.0),
            bar(45, 108.0, 111.0, 94.0, 110.5),
        ]
    )
    trade = simulate_position(SPEC, bars, 0, long_signal())
    assert trade.exit_reason == "stop"
    assert trade.exit_resolution == "no_minutes"
    assert trade.exit_price == pytest.approx(94.5)
    assert trade.gross_pnl == pytest.approx(-7.0)


def test_gap_through_stop_fills_at_open():
    bars = make_bars(
        [
            bar(0, 99.0, 101.0, 98.0, 100.0),
            bar(15, 101.0, 103.0, 100.0, 102.0),
            bar(30, 102.0, 106.0, 101.0, 105.0),
            bar(45, 90.0, 92.0, 88.0, 89.0),
        ]
    )
    trade = simulate_position(SPEC, bars, 0, long_signal())
    assert trade.exit_reason == "stop"
    assert trade.exit_price == pytest.approx(89.5)
    assert trade.gross_pnl == pytest.approx(-12.0)


def test_stop_on_entry_bar():
    bars = make_bars(
        [
            bar(0, 99.0, 101.0, 98.0, 100.0),
            bar(15, 101.0, 102.0, 94.0, 95.0),
            bar(30, 96.0, 97.0, 95.5, 96.0),
        ]
    )
    trade = simulate_position(SPEC, bars, 0, long_signal())
    assert trade.exit_reason == "stop"
    assert trade.bars_held == 1
    assert trade.exit_price == pytest.approx(94.5)
    assert trade.gross_pnl == pytest.approx(-7.0)


def test_time_exit_at_close():
    bars = make_bars(
        [
            bar(0, 99.0, 101.0, 98.0, 100.0),
            bar(15, 101.0, 103.0, 100.0, 102.0),
            bar(30, 102.0, 106.0, 101.0, 105.0),
            bar(45, 106.0, 109.0, 105.0, 108.0),
        ]
    )
    trade = simulate_position(SPEC, bars, 0, long_signal(max_bars=2))
    assert trade.exit_reason == "time"
    assert trade.bars_held == 2
    assert trade.exit_price == pytest.approx(104.5)
    assert trade.gross_pnl == pytest.approx(3.0)


def with_marks(
    bars: pl.DataFrame,
    day_end: set[int] = frozenset(),
    before_break: set[int] = frozenset(),
) -> pl.DataFrame:
    """Отметки расписания по номерам баров; перерыв — тоже конец дня."""
    return (
        bars.with_row_index("_i")
        .with_columns(
            pl.col("_i").is_in(day_end | before_break).alias("is_day_end"),
            pl.col("_i").is_in(before_break).alias("is_before_break"),
        )
        .drop("_i")
    )


def calendar_bars() -> pl.DataFrame:
    """Цена растёт между стопом 95 и целью 110: уровни не задеты."""
    return make_bars(
        [
            bar(0, 99.0, 101.0, 98.0, 100.0),
            bar(15, 101.0, 103.0, 100.0, 102.0),
            bar(30, 102.0, 106.0, 101.0, 105.0),
            bar(45, 106.0, 109.0, 105.0, 108.0),
            bar(60, 108.0, 109.0, 106.0, 107.0),
        ]
    )


def test_hold_day_exits_at_close_of_last_bar_of_day():
    bars = with_marks(calendar_bars(), day_end={2})
    trade = simulate_position(SPEC, bars, 0, long_signal(hold="day"))
    assert trade.exit_reason == "day_end"
    assert trade.exit_time == START + timedelta(minutes=30)
    assert trade.bars_held == 2
    # Вход 101 + 0.5, выход по закрытию 105 − 0.5.
    assert trade.exit_price == pytest.approx(104.5)
    assert trade.gross_pnl == pytest.approx(3.0)
    # Последний бар виден целиком: high 106 против входа 101.5.
    assert trade.mfe == pytest.approx(4.5)


def test_hold_overnight_passes_ordinary_night_and_exits_before_break():
    bars = with_marks(calendar_bars(), day_end={1}, before_break={3})
    trade = simulate_position(SPEC, bars, 0, long_signal(hold="overnight"))
    assert trade.exit_reason == "break"
    assert trade.exit_time == START + timedelta(minutes=45)
    assert trade.exit_price == pytest.approx(107.5)


def test_hold_day_exits_before_break_too():
    bars = with_marks(calendar_bars(), before_break={2})
    trade = simulate_position(SPEC, bars, 0, long_signal(hold="day"))
    assert trade.exit_reason == "day_end"
    assert trade.exit_time == START + timedelta(minutes=30)


def test_hold_any_ignores_marks():
    bars = with_marks(calendar_bars(), day_end={1}, before_break={2})
    trade = simulate_position(SPEC, bars, 0, long_signal())
    assert trade.exit_reason == "end"
    assert trade.exit_time == START + timedelta(minutes=60)


def test_entry_on_last_bar_of_day_exits_on_the_same_bar():
    bars = with_marks(calendar_bars(), day_end={1})
    trade = simulate_position(SPEC, bars, 0, long_signal(hold="day"))
    assert trade.exit_reason == "day_end"
    assert trade.bars_held == 1
    # Вход по открытию 101 + 0.5, выход по закрытию 102 − 0.5.
    assert trade.gross_pnl == pytest.approx(0.0)


def test_stop_inside_last_bar_of_day_comes_before_schedule_exit():
    bars = with_marks(
        make_bars(
            [
                bar(0, 99.0, 101.0, 98.0, 100.0),
                bar(15, 101.0, 103.0, 100.0, 102.0),
                bar(30, 102.0, 103.0, 94.0, 101.0),
            ]
        ),
        day_end={2},
    )
    trade = simulate_position(SPEC, bars, 0, long_signal(hold="day"))
    assert trade.exit_reason == "stop"
    assert trade.exit_price == pytest.approx(94.5)


def test_max_bars_on_the_same_bar_is_reported_as_time():
    bars = with_marks(calendar_bars(), day_end={2})
    trade = simulate_position(SPEC, bars, 0, long_signal(hold="day", max_bars=2))
    assert trade.exit_reason == "time"


def test_schedule_exit_keeps_position_out_of_the_gap():
    # После последнего бара дня цена открывается далеко за стопом.
    rows = [
        bar(0, 99.0, 101.0, 98.0, 100.0),
        bar(15, 101.0, 103.0, 100.0, 102.0),
        bar(30, 102.0, 103.0, 101.0, 102.0),
        bar(45, 80.0, 81.0, 79.0, 80.0),
    ]
    bars = with_marks(make_bars(rows), before_break={2})
    held = simulate_position(SPEC, bars, 0, long_signal())
    flat = simulate_position(SPEC, bars, 0, long_signal(hold="overnight"))
    assert held.exit_reason == "stop"
    assert held.gross_pnl == pytest.approx(79.5 - 101.5)
    assert flat.exit_reason == "break"
    assert flat.gross_pnl == pytest.approx(101.5 - 101.5)


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("hold", "column"), [("day", "is_day_end"), ("overnight", "is_before_break")]
)
def test_hold_needs_schedule_marks(hold, column):
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match=column):
        simulate_position(SPEC, calendar_bars(), 0, long_signal(hold=hold))


def test_unknown_hold_rejected():
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="hold"):
        long_signal(hold="weekend")


# ---------------------------------------------------------- подвижный стоп

# Без издержек: цены входа и выхода равны уровням, результат — в пунктах.
FREE = ContractSpec(
    asset="TEST", tick_size=0.1, exchange_fee_rate=0.0, slippage_ticks=0.0
)


def test_breakeven_moves_stop_from_the_next_bar():
    bars = make_bars(
        [
            bar(0, 99.5, 100.5, 99.5, 100.0),
            # Касание +1R (101.0): стоп переедет на вход 100, но со следующего бара.
            bar(15, 100.0, 101.2, 99.3, 100.5),
            bar(30, 100.4, 100.6, 99.8, 100.2),
            bar(45, 100.2, 100.4, 100.0, 100.3),
        ]
    )
    signal = Signal(side=LONG, stop=99.0, target=103.0, breakeven_r=1.0)
    trade = simulate_position(FREE, bars, 0, signal)
    assert trade.exit_reason == "breakeven"
    assert trade.exit_time == START + timedelta(minutes=30)
    assert trade.exit_price == pytest.approx(100.0)
    assert trade.gross_pnl == pytest.approx(0.0)


def test_breakeven_does_not_help_inside_the_same_bar():
    # Бар дошёл до +1.2R и упал ниже начального стопа: что было раньше,
    # неизвестно, поэтому в этом баре стоп ещё начальный.
    bars = make_bars(
        [
            bar(0, 99.5, 100.5, 99.5, 100.0),
            bar(15, 100.0, 101.2, 98.9, 99.2),
            bar(30, 99.2, 99.5, 99.0, 99.4),
        ]
    )
    signal = Signal(side=LONG, stop=99.0, target=103.0, breakeven_r=1.0)
    trade = simulate_position(FREE, bars, 0, signal)
    assert trade.exit_reason == "stop"
    assert trade.exit_price == pytest.approx(99.0)
    assert trade.gross_pnl == pytest.approx(-1.0)


def test_breakeven_needs_the_threshold_to_be_touched():
    bars = make_bars(
        [
            bar(0, 99.5, 100.5, 99.5, 100.0),
            bar(15, 100.0, 100.9, 99.6, 100.5),
            bar(30, 100.4, 100.6, 99.8, 100.2),
        ]
    )
    signal = Signal(side=LONG, stop=99.0, target=103.0, breakeven_r=1.0)
    trade = simulate_position(FREE, bars, 0, signal)
    # 100.9 < 101.0: стоп остался 99, минимум 99.8 его не задел.
    assert trade.exit_reason == "end"


def trailing_rows() -> list[dict]:
    """Лонг от 100: лучшее закрытие 103, потом откат."""
    return [
        bar(0, 99.5, 100.5, 99.5, 100.0),
        bar(15, 100.0, 101.3, 99.6, 101.0),  # стоп → 101 − 1.5 = 99.5
        bar(30, 101.0, 103.4, 100.8, 103.0),  # стоп → 101.5
        bar(45, 103.0, 103.2, 101.6, 102.0),  # лучшее закрытие то же: стоп 101.5
        bar(60, 102.0, 102.4, 101.0, 101.2),  # задевает 101.5
    ]


def test_trailing_stop_follows_best_close_and_never_loosens():
    signal = Signal(side=LONG, stop=98.5, target=None, trail=1.5)
    trade = simulate_position(FREE, make_bars(trailing_rows()), 0, signal)
    assert trade.exit_reason == "trail"
    assert trade.exit_time == START + timedelta(minutes=60)
    # Не 102 − 1.5 = 100.5: после отката стоп назад не отходит.
    assert trade.exit_price == pytest.approx(101.5)
    assert trade.gross_pnl == pytest.approx(1.5)


def test_gap_through_trailing_stop_exits_at_open():
    rows = trailing_rows()
    rows[3] = bar(45, 100.0, 100.5, 99.5, 100.2)
    signal = Signal(side=LONG, stop=98.5, target=None, trail=1.5)
    trade = simulate_position(FREE, make_bars(rows[:4]), 0, signal)
    assert trade.exit_reason == "trail"
    assert trade.exit_price == pytest.approx(100.0)


def test_short_trailing_stop_mirrors_long():
    mirrored = [
        {
            **row,
            "open": 200.0 - row["open"],
            "high": 200.0 - row["low"],
            "low": 200.0 - row["high"],
            "close": 200.0 - row["close"],
        }
        for row in trailing_rows()
    ]
    signal = Signal(side=SHORT, stop=101.5, target=None, trail=1.5)
    trade = simulate_position(FREE, make_bars(mirrored), 0, signal)
    assert trade.exit_reason == "trail"
    assert trade.exit_price == pytest.approx(98.5)
    assert trade.gross_pnl == pytest.approx(1.5)


def test_trailing_stop_does_not_jump_on_contract_roll():
    # Новый контракт на 30 дороже с бара 30: реальные цены другие, а
    # непрерывные те же — результат тот же, что без переноса.
    rolled = [
        {
            **row,
            "open": row["open"] + 30.0,
            "high": row["high"] + 30.0,
            "low": row["low"] + 30.0,
            "close": row["close"] + 30.0,
            "price_adjustment": -30.0,
            "is_first_bar_after_roll": index == 2,
        }
        if index >= 2
        else row
        for index, row in enumerate(trailing_rows())
    ]
    signal = Signal(side=LONG, stop=98.5, target=None, trail=1.5)
    plain = simulate_position(FREE, make_bars(trailing_rows()), 0, signal)
    trade = simulate_position(FREE, make_bars(rolled), 0, signal)
    assert trade.exit_reason == "trail"
    assert trade.exit_time == plain.exit_time
    assert trade.exit_price == pytest.approx(plain.exit_price + 30.0)
    assert trade.gross_pnl == pytest.approx(plain.gross_pnl)


def test_no_target_exits_by_stop_or_schedule():
    rows = [
        bar(0, 99.5, 100.5, 99.5, 100.0),
        bar(15, 100.0, 150.0, 99.0, 140.0),
        bar(30, 140.0, 160.0, 139.0, 150.0),
    ]
    signal = Signal(side=LONG, stop=95.0, target=None, hold="overnight")
    trade = simulate_position(
        FREE, with_marks(make_bars(rows), before_break={1}), 0, signal
    )
    assert trade.exit_reason == "break"
    assert trade.exit_price == pytest.approx(140.0)
    without_schedule = simulate_position(
        FREE, make_bars(rows), 0, Signal(side=LONG, stop=95.0, target=None)
    )
    assert without_schedule.exit_reason == "end"


def test_future_bars_do_not_change_trade_closed_by_trailing_stop():
    rows = trailing_rows() + [bar(75, 101.0, 105.0, 100.0, 104.0)]
    signal = Signal(side=LONG, stop=98.5, target=None, trail=1.5)
    trade = simulate_position(FREE, make_bars(rows), 0, signal)
    changed = rows[:5] + [bar(75, 50.0, 60.0, 40.0, 55.0)]
    assert simulate_position(FREE, make_bars(changed), 0, signal) == trade


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "kwargs",
    [
        {"breakeven_r": 0.0},
        {"trail": -1.0},
        {"target": 0.0},
        # Безубыток считается от стопа: без стопа его нет.
        {"stop": None, "breakeven_r": 1.0},
    ],
)
def test_bad_moving_stop_settings_rejected(kwargs):
    params = {"side": LONG, "stop": 95.0, "target": 110.0, **kwargs}
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        Signal(**params)


def test_stop_must_be_below_close_even_without_target():
    bars = make_bars([bar(0, 99.5, 100.5, 99.5, 100.0), bar(15, 100, 101, 99, 100)])
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="лонга"):
        simulate_position(FREE, bars, 0, Signal(side=LONG, stop=101.0, target=None))


def test_moving_stops_charge_costs_exactly_once():
    # Подвижный стоп считается по ценам рынка, а не исполнения: выход тот же,
    # что на рынке без издержек, и «до издержек» равно его результату.
    rng = random.Random(17)
    reasons = set()
    for _ in range(300):
        bars = random_trade_bars(rng)
        side = rng.choice((LONG, SHORT))
        distance = rng.uniform(1.0, 6.0)
        signal = Signal(
            side=side,
            stop=100.0 - side * distance,
            target=rng.choice([None, 100.0 + side * rng.uniform(2.0, 12.0)]),
            contracts=rng.randint(1, 5),
            breakeven_r=rng.choice([None, 0.5, 1.0]),
            trail=rng.choice([None, distance, distance / 2]),
        )
        trade = simulate_position(SPEC, bars, 0, signal)
        no_costs = simulate_position(FREE, bars, 0, signal)
        assert trade.exit_time == no_costs.exit_time
        assert trade.exit_reason == no_costs.exit_reason
        assert trade.pnl_before_costs == pytest.approx(no_costs.net_pnl)
        assert trade.path[-1][1] == pytest.approx(trade.net_pnl)
        assert trade.mae <= 0.0 <= trade.mfe
        reasons.add(trade.exit_reason)
    assert {"stop", "breakeven", "trail", "target", "end"} <= reasons


def test_short_target_mirrors_long():
    bars = make_bars(
        [
            bar(0, 99.0, 101.0, 98.0, 100.0),
            bar(15, 99.0, 101.0, 98.0, 98.0),
            bar(30, 97.0, 99.0, 95.0, 96.0),
            bar(45, 93.0, 94.0, 88.0, 89.0),
        ]
    )
    trade = simulate_position(
        SPEC, bars, 0, Signal(side=SHORT, stop=105.0, target=90.0)
    )
    assert trade.side == SHORT
    assert trade.entry_price == pytest.approx(98.5)
    assert trade.exit_price == pytest.approx(90.5)
    assert trade.exit_reason == "target"
    assert trade.gross_pnl == pytest.approx(8.0)
    assert trade.commissions == pytest.approx(0.0189)


def test_roll_transfer_hides_gap_and_charges_cost():
    bars = make_bars(
        [
            bar(0, 99.5, 100.5, 99.0, 100.0),
            bar(15, 100.0, 100.5, 99.5, 100.0),
            bar(30, 10.0, 10.6, 9.9, 10.5, adjustment=90.0, roll=True),
            bar(45, 10.5, 11.0, 10.4, 11.0, adjustment=90.0),
            bar(60, 11.0, 11.2, 10.8, 11.0, adjustment=90.0),
        ]
    )
    trade = simulate_position(SPEC, bars, 0, long_signal(target=200.0, max_bars=4))
    assert trade.entry_price == pytest.approx(100.5)
    assert trade.exit_price == pytest.approx(10.5)
    assert trade.exit_reason == "time"
    assert trade.bars_held == 4
    assert trade.gross_pnl == pytest.approx(0.0)
    assert trade.roll_costs == pytest.approx(1.011)
    assert trade.commissions == pytest.approx(0.0111)
    assert trade.net_pnl == pytest.approx(-1.0221)


def test_roll_keeps_overnight_gap_through_stop():
    # Базис 90 убран поправкой, но за ночь рынок упал на 5 пунктов
    # (100 -> 95 в adjusted) и выбил стоп 97: убыток гэпа остаётся в P&L.
    bars = make_bars(
        [
            bar(0, 99.5, 100.5, 99.0, 100.0),
            bar(15, 100.0, 100.5, 99.5, 100.0),
            bar(30, 5.0, 5.5, 4.0, 5.0, adjustment=90.0, roll=True),
        ]
    )
    trade = simulate_position(SPEC, bars, 0, long_signal(stop=97.0))
    assert trade.exit_reason == "stop"
    assert trade.bars_held == 2
    assert trade.exit_price == pytest.approx(4.5)
    assert trade.gross_pnl == pytest.approx(-6.0)
    assert trade.roll_costs == pytest.approx(1.0105)


def test_roll_keeps_overnight_gain():
    bars = make_bars(
        [
            bar(0, 99.5, 100.5, 99.0, 100.0),
            bar(15, 100.0, 100.5, 99.5, 100.0),
            bar(30, 13.0, 14.5, 12.5, 14.0, adjustment=90.0, roll=True),
        ]
    )
    trade = simulate_position(SPEC, bars, 0, long_signal(max_bars=2))
    assert trade.exit_reason == "time"
    assert trade.exit_price == pytest.approx(13.5)
    assert trade.gross_pnl == pytest.approx(3.0)
    assert trade.roll_costs == pytest.approx(1.0113)


def test_negative_adjusted_prices_do_not_change_result():
    # Аддитивная поправка может увести adjusted-цены ниже нуля (как у NG).
    # Результат сделки от уровня поправки не зависит.
    rows = [
        (0, 99.0, 101.0, 98.0, 100.0),
        (15, 101.0, 103.0, 100.0, 102.0),
        (30, 102.0, 106.0, 101.0, 105.0),
        (45, 108.0, 111.0, 107.0, 110.0),
    ]
    plain = simulate_position(
        SPEC, make_bars([bar(*row) for row in rows]), 0, long_signal()
    )
    shifted = simulate_position(
        SPEC,
        make_bars([bar(*row, adjustment=-150.0) for row in rows]),
        0,
        long_signal(),
    )
    assert shifted.entry_price == pytest.approx(plain.entry_price)
    assert shifted.exit_price == pytest.approx(plain.exit_price)
    assert shifted.exit_reason == plain.exit_reason
    assert shifted.gross_pnl == pytest.approx(plain.gross_pnl)
    assert shifted.net_pnl == pytest.approx(plain.net_pnl)


def test_future_perturbation_does_not_change_result():
    rows = [
        bar(0, 99.0, 101.0, 98.0, 100.0),
        bar(15, 101.0, 103.0, 100.0, 102.0),
        bar(30, 102.0, 111.0, 101.0, 108.0),
        bar(45, 108.0, 109.0, 107.0, 108.0),
        bar(60, 108.0, 110.0, 107.0, 109.0),
        bar(75, 109.0, 112.0, 108.0, 111.0),
    ]
    trade = simulate_position(SPEC, make_bars(rows), 0, long_signal())
    perturbed = [
        {
            **row,
            "open": row["open"] * 2,
            "high": row["high"] * 2,
            "low": row["low"] * 2,
            "close": row["close"] * 2,
        }
        if index > 2
        else row
        for index, row in enumerate(rows)
    ]
    trade_perturbed = simulate_position(SPEC, make_bars(perturbed), 0, long_signal())
    assert trade == trade_perturbed


def test_end_of_data_closes_position():
    bars = make_bars(
        [
            bar(0, 99.0, 101.0, 98.0, 100.0),
            bar(15, 101.0, 103.0, 100.0, 102.0),
            bar(30, 103.0, 104.0, 102.0, 103.0),
        ]
    )
    trade = simulate_position(SPEC, bars, 0, long_signal(target=200.0))
    assert trade.exit_reason == "end"
    assert trade.exit_price == pytest.approx(102.5)


def test_open_position_without_end_exit_is_rejected():
    bars = make_bars(
        [
            bar(0, 99.0, 101.0, 98.0, 100.0),
            bar(15, 101.0, 103.0, 100.0, 102.0),
            bar(30, 103.0, 104.0, 102.0, 103.0),
        ]
    )
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="осталась открытой"):
        simulate_position(SPEC, bars, 0, long_signal(target=200.0), exit_at_end=False)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("signal_bar", [-1, 2])
def test_signal_needs_a_next_bar(signal_bar):
    bars = make_bars(
        [
            bar(0, 99.0, 101.0, 98.0, 100.0),
            bar(15, 101.0, 103.0, 100.0, 102.0),
            bar(30, 103.0, 104.0, 102.0, 103.0),
        ]
    )
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="следующий бар"):
        simulate_position(SPEC, bars, signal_bar, long_signal())


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "signal",
    [
        Signal(side=LONG, stop=100.0, target=110.0),
        Signal(side=LONG, stop=95.0, target=100.0),
        Signal(side=SHORT, stop=100.0, target=90.0),
        Signal(side=SHORT, stop=105.0, target=100.0),
    ],
)
def test_levels_must_bracket_signal_close(signal):
    bars = make_bars(
        [
            bar(0, 99.0, 101.0, 98.0, 100.0),
            bar(15, 101.0, 103.0, 100.0, 102.0),
        ]
    )
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="требуется"):
        simulate_position(SPEC, bars, 0, signal)


def test_missing_columns_rejected():
    bars = pl.DataFrame({"timestamp": [datetime(2026, 9, 10)], "open": [100.0]})
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="нет столбцов"):
        simulate_position(SPEC, bars, 0, long_signal())


def test_long_gap_up_past_target_fills_target_at_open_not_stop():
    bars = make_bars(
        [
            bar(0, 100.0, 101.0, 99.0, 100.0),
            bar(15, 115.0, 120.0, 85.0, 90.0),
        ]
    )
    trade = simulate_position(SPEC, bars, 0, long_signal(stop=90.0, target=110.0))
    assert trade.exit_reason == "target"
    assert trade.exit_price == pytest.approx(114.5)
    assert trade.gross_pnl == pytest.approx(-1.0)
    assert trade.net_pnl == pytest.approx(-1.0 - 0.023)


def test_short_gap_down_past_target_fills_target_at_open_not_stop():
    bars = make_bars(
        [
            bar(0, 100.0, 101.0, 99.0, 100.0),
            bar(15, 85.0, 115.0, 80.0, 110.0),
        ]
    )
    trade = simulate_position(
        SPEC, bars, 0, Signal(side=SHORT, stop=110.0, target=90.0)
    )
    assert trade.exit_reason == "target"
    assert trade.exit_price == pytest.approx(85.5)
    assert trade.gross_pnl == pytest.approx(-1.0)
    assert trade.net_pnl == pytest.approx(-1.0 - 0.017)


def test_entry_open_gap_through_stop_exits_on_entry_bar():
    bars = make_bars(
        [
            bar(0, 100.0, 101.0, 99.0, 100.0),
            bar(15, 90.0, 91.0, 89.0, 90.5),
        ]
    )
    trade = simulate_position(SPEC, bars, 0, long_signal(stop=95.0, target=110.0))
    assert trade.exit_reason == "stop"
    assert trade.bars_held == 1
    assert trade.exit_price == pytest.approx(89.5)
    assert trade.gross_pnl == pytest.approx(-1.0)


# Рубли за пункт по барам: Brent — $10 за пункт × курс клиринга этого бара.
# Курс 80 — 800 ₽ за пункт, курс 90 — 900 ₽. Бар 1440 — следующий день.

BRENT_NO_COSTS = ContractSpec(
    asset="BRENT", tick_size=0.01, exchange_fee_rate=0.0, slippage_ticks=0.0
)


def test_profit_uses_point_value_of_each_bar():
    # +$1 в первый день при курсе 80 и ещё +$1 во второй при курсе 90.
    bars = make_bars(
        [
            bar(0, 70.0, 70.0, 70.0, 70.0, point_value=800.0),
            bar(15, 70.0, 71.0, 70.0, 71.0, point_value=800.0),
            bar(1440, 71.0, 72.0, 71.0, 72.0, point_value=900.0),
        ]
    )
    signal = long_signal(stop=60.0, target=80.0, max_bars=2)
    trade = simulate_position(BRENT_NO_COSTS, bars, 0, signal)
    assert trade.gross_pnl == pytest.approx(1 * 800 + 1 * 900)
    assert trade.net_pnl == pytest.approx(1_700.0)

    # «Одно число» по последнему курсу переоценило бы и первый день.
    single = bars.with_columns(pl.lit(900.0).alias("point_value"))
    trade_single = simulate_position(BRENT_NO_COSTS, single, 0, signal)
    assert trade_single.gross_pnl == pytest.approx(2 * 900)


def test_flat_price_with_rising_rate_gives_zero_profit():
    # Рублёвая стоимость контракта выросла с 56 000 до 63 000 ₽, но биржа
    # платит только за изменение цены: вариационная маржа равна нулю.
    bars = make_bars(
        [
            bar(0, 70.0, 70.0, 70.0, 70.0, point_value=800.0),
            bar(15, 70.0, 70.0, 70.0, 70.0, point_value=800.0),
            bar(1440, 70.0, 70.0, 70.0, 70.0, point_value=850.0),
            bar(2880, 70.0, 70.0, 70.0, 70.0, point_value=900.0),
        ]
    )
    signal = long_signal(stop=60.0, target=80.0, max_bars=3)
    trade = simulate_position(BRENT_NO_COSTS, bars, 0, signal)
    assert trade.bars_held == 3
    assert trade.gross_pnl == pytest.approx(0.0)
    assert trade.net_pnl == pytest.approx(0.0)


def test_costs_use_point_value_of_their_bar():
    # Вход при 800 ₽ за пункт, перенос при 850, выход при 900.
    # Новый контракт дешевле старого на $1 (поправка 1.0).
    fee = 0.000132
    spec = ContractSpec(asset="BRENT", tick_size=0.01, exchange_fee_rate=fee)
    bars = make_bars(
        [
            bar(0, 70.00, 70.10, 69.90, 70.00, point_value=800.0),
            bar(15, 70.00, 70.60, 69.95, 70.50, point_value=800.0),
            bar(
                1440,
                69.00,
                69.60,
                68.95,
                69.50,
                adjustment=1.0,
                roll=True,
                contract="TSH7",
                point_value=850.0,
            ),
            bar(
                2880,
                69.50,
                70.10,
                69.45,
                70.00,
                adjustment=1.0,
                contract="TSH7",
                point_value=900.0,
            ),
        ]
    )
    signal = long_signal(stop=65.0, target=75.0, max_bars=3)
    trade = simulate_position(spec, bars, 0, signal)
    assert trade.entry_price == pytest.approx(70.01)
    assert trade.exit_price == pytest.approx(69.99)
    # Вход 70.01 → закрытие 70.50 при 800; перенос без движения; 70.50 → 70.99
    # (выход 69.99 + поправка 1.0) при 900.
    assert trade.gross_pnl == pytest.approx(0.49 * 800 + 0.49 * 900)
    assert trade.commissions == pytest.approx((70.01 * 800 + 69.99 * 900) * fee)
    # Две ноги проскальзывания по $0.01 и две комиссии — всё при 850.
    expected_roll = 2 * 0.01 * 850 + (70.50 + 69.00) * 850 * fee
    assert trade.roll_costs == pytest.approx(expected_roll)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("name", ["cnyrub", "mix"])
def test_rub_markets_keep_single_point_value_result(name):
    # У рублёвых рынков рубли за пункт постоянны (CNY 1000 ₽, MIX 1 ₽):
    # результат по барам совпадает с прежней формулой «одно число k».
    # Шаг цены условный.
    market = MARKETS[name]
    k = market.quote_point_value
    fee = market.exchange_fee_rate
    bars = add_point_value(
        make_bars(
            [
                bar(0, 99.5, 100.5, 99.0, 100.0),
                bar(15, 100.0, 100.5, 99.5, 100.0),
                bar(30, 13.0, 14.5, 12.5, 14.0, adjustment=90.0, roll=True),
            ]
        ),
        market,
    )
    spec = ContractSpec(asset=name, tick_size=0.5, exchange_fee_rate=fee)
    trade = simulate_position(spec, bars, 0, long_signal(contracts=3, max_bars=2))
    assert trade.entry_price == pytest.approx(100.5)
    assert trade.exit_price == pytest.approx(13.5)
    assert trade.gross_pnl == pytest.approx((13.5 + 90.0 - 100.5) * k * 3)
    assert trade.commissions == pytest.approx((100.5 + 13.5) * k * 3 * fee)
    assert trade.roll_costs == pytest.approx(
        2 * 0.5 * k * 3 + (100.0 + 13.0) * k * 3 * fee
    )


@pytest.mark.parametrize("point_value", [None, 0.0])
def test_bar_without_point_value_is_rejected(point_value):
    bars = make_bars(
        [
            bar(0, 99.0, 101.0, 98.0, 100.0),
            bar(15, 101.0, 103.0, 100.0, 102.0, point_value=point_value),
        ]
    )
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="point_value"):
        simulate_position(SPEC, bars, 0, long_signal())


# Бар задел и стоп, и цель: порядок касаний берётся из минуток того же контракта.


def write_minutes(
    directory: Path,
    contract: str,
    bar_minute: int,
    prices: list[tuple[float, float, float, float]],
) -> RawMinutes:
    """Минутки одного 15-минутного бара в формате raw: (open, high, low, close)."""
    start = START + timedelta(minutes=bar_minute)
    pl.DataFrame(
        [
            {
                "timestamp": start + timedelta(minutes=i),
                "open": open_,
                "high": high,
                "low": low,
                "close": close,
            }
            for i, (open_, high, low, close) in enumerate(prices)
        ]
    ).write_parquet(directory / f"{contract}.parquet")
    return RawMinutes(directory)


def helicopter_bars() -> pl.DataFrame:
    """Сигнал на 100; бар входа задел и стоп 95, и цель 110."""
    return make_bars(
        [
            bar(0, 99.0, 101.0, 98.0, 100.0),
            bar(15, 101.0, 111.0, 94.0, 100.0),
        ]
    )


def test_minutes_show_target_was_first(tmp_path):
    minutes = write_minutes(
        tmp_path,
        "TSZ6",
        15,
        [
            (101.0, 105.0, 100.0, 104.0),
            (104.0, 111.0, 103.0, 108.0),  # 10:16 — цель 110
            (108.0, 108.0, 94.0, 96.0),  # 10:17 — стоп 95, уже поздно
            (96.0, 101.0, 95.5, 100.0),
        ],
    )
    trade = simulate_position(
        SPEC, helicopter_bars(), 0, long_signal(), minutes=minutes
    )
    assert trade.exit_reason == "target"
    assert trade.exit_resolution == "minutes"
    assert trade.exit_price == pytest.approx(109.5)
    assert trade.gross_pnl == pytest.approx(8.0)


def test_minutes_show_stop_was_first(tmp_path):
    minutes = write_minutes(
        tmp_path,
        "TSZ6",
        15,
        [
            (101.0, 102.0, 100.0, 100.5),
            (100.5, 100.5, 94.0, 95.5),  # 10:16 — стоп 95
            (95.5, 111.0, 95.5, 108.0),  # 10:17 — цель 110, уже поздно
            (108.0, 108.0, 99.0, 100.0),
        ],
    )
    trade = simulate_position(
        SPEC, helicopter_bars(), 0, long_signal(), minutes=minutes
    )
    assert trade.exit_reason == "stop"
    assert trade.exit_resolution == "minutes"
    assert trade.exit_price == pytest.approx(94.5)
    assert trade.gross_pnl == pytest.approx(-7.0)


def test_both_levels_in_one_minute_fall_back_to_stop(tmp_path):
    minutes = write_minutes(
        tmp_path,
        "TSZ6",
        15,
        [
            (101.0, 102.0, 100.0, 101.0),
            (101.0, 111.0, 94.0, 100.0),  # 10:16 — и стоп, и цель
        ],
    )
    trade = simulate_position(
        SPEC, helicopter_bars(), 0, long_signal(), minutes=minutes
    )
    assert trade.exit_reason == "stop"
    assert trade.exit_resolution == "same_minute"
    assert trade.exit_price == pytest.approx(94.5)


def test_minutes_not_matching_bar_fall_back_to_stop(tmp_path):
    # Максимум минуток 109, а у бара 111: минутки описывают не этот бар.
    minutes = write_minutes(tmp_path, "TSZ6", 15, [(101.0, 109.0, 94.0, 100.0)])
    trade = simulate_position(
        SPEC, helicopter_bars(), 0, long_signal(), minutes=minutes
    )
    assert trade.exit_reason == "stop"
    assert trade.exit_resolution == "minutes_mismatch"
    assert trade.exit_price == pytest.approx(94.5)


def test_minute_opening_beyond_stop_exits_at_its_open(tmp_path):
    # 10:16 открылась на 93, сразу за стопом 95: как и у 15-минутного бара,
    # выход по цене гэпа, хотя позже в той же минутке цена дошла до цели.
    bars = make_bars(
        [
            bar(0, 99.0, 101.0, 98.0, 100.0),
            bar(15, 101.0, 111.0, 93.0, 108.0),
        ]
    )
    minutes = write_minutes(
        tmp_path,
        "TSZ6",
        15,
        [(101.0, 102.0, 100.0, 100.0), (93.0, 111.0, 93.0, 108.0)],
    )
    trade = simulate_position(SPEC, bars, 0, long_signal(), minutes=minutes)
    assert trade.exit_reason == "stop"
    assert trade.exit_resolution == "minutes"
    assert trade.exit_price == pytest.approx(92.5)
    assert trade.gross_pnl == pytest.approx(-9.0)


def test_short_minutes_show_target_was_first(tmp_path):

            (91.0, 106.0, 91.0, 104.0),  # 10:17 — стоп 105, уже поздно
            (104.0, 104.0, 99.0, 100.0),
        ],
    )
    trade = simulate_position(
        SPEC, bars, 0, Signal(side=SHORT, stop=105.0, target=90.0), minutes=minutes

    assert trade.exit_resolution == "minutes"
    assert trade.exit_price == pytest.approx(90.5)
    assert trade.gross_pnl == pytest.approx(8.0)


def test_minutes_after_roll_use_new_contract_and_adjustment(tmp_path):
    # Новый контракт дешевле на 90: стоп 95 и цель 110 в его ценах — 5 и 20.
    # Файл минуток есть только у нового контракта TSH7.
    bars = make_bars(
        [
            bar(0, 99.5, 100.5, 99.0, 100.0),
            bar(15, 100.0, 100.5, 99.5, 100.0),
            bar(30, 10.0, 21.0, 4.0, 10.0, adjustment=90.0, roll=True, contract="TSH7"),
        ]
    )
    minutes = write_minutes(
        tmp_path,
        "TSH7",
        30,
        [
            (10.0, 15.0, 9.0, 14.0),
            (14.0, 21.0, 13.0, 18.0),  # 10:31 — цель 20
            (18.0, 18.0, 4.0, 10.0),  # 10:32 — стоп 5, уже поздно
        ],
    )
    trade = simulate_position(SPEC, bars, 0, long_signal(), minutes=minutes)
    assert trade.exit_reason == "target"
    assert trade.exit_resolution == "minutes"
    assert trade.exit_price == pytest.approx(19.5)
    assert trade.gross_pnl == pytest.approx(9.0)
    assert trade.roll_costs == pytest.approx(1.011)


def test_minutes_are_not_read_for_bars_without_level_inside(tmp_path):
    # Каталог минуток пуст. Бары без касания и бар, открывшийся за целью,
    # минутки не запрашивают: выход по открытию и так однозначен.
    bars = make_bars(
        [
            bar(0, 99.0, 101.0, 98.0, 100.0),
            bar(15, 101.0, 103.0, 100.0, 102.0),
            bar(30, 111.0, 112.0, 107.0, 110.0),
        ]
    )
    trade = simulate_position(
        SPEC, bars, 0, long_signal(), minutes=RawMinutes(tmp_path)
    )
    assert trade.exit_reason == "target"
    assert trade.exit_resolution == "bar"
    assert trade.exit_price == pytest.approx(110.5)


def one_level_bars() -> pl.DataFrame:
    """Сигнал на 100, стоп 95; бар входа задел только стоп (минимум 93)."""
    return make_bars(
        [
            bar(0, 99.0, 101.0, 98.0, 100.0),
            bar(15, 101.0, 102.0, 93.0, 94.0),
        ]
    )


def test_minute_opening_beyond_stop_fills_at_its_open_in_one_level_bar(tmp_path):
    minutes = write_minutes(
        tmp_path,
        "TSZ6",
        15,
        [
            (101.0, 102.0, 99.0, 99.5),
            (99.5, 99.8, 96.0, 96.2),
            (94.0, 94.5, 93.0, 94.0),  # открылась за стопом 95
        ],
    )
    trade = simulate_position(SPEC, one_level_bars(), 0, long_signal(), minutes=minutes)
    assert trade.exit_reason == "stop"
    assert trade.exit_resolution == "minutes"
    # 94 − 0.5 проскальзывания, а не 95 − 0.5, как по одному бару.
    assert trade.exit_price == pytest.approx(93.5)
    without = simulate_position(SPEC, one_level_bars(), 0, long_signal())
    assert without.exit_resolution == "bar"
    assert without.exit_price == pytest.approx(94.5)


def test_minute_touching_stop_inside_fills_at_stop(tmp_path):
    minutes = write_minutes(
        tmp_path,
        "TSZ6",

        [
            (101.0, 102.0, 99.0, 99.5),

        ],
    )
    trade = simulate_position(SPEC, one_level_bars(), 0, long_signal(), minutes=minutes)
    assert trade.exit_resolution == "minutes"
    assert trade.exit_price == pytest.approx(94.5)

def test_moved_stop_fill_uses_minutes_too(tmp_path):

        tmp_path,
        "TSZ6",
        60,

    )
